Cloud Linux after the chat-write door slice: production chat POST, generation SSE, cancellation and attachments return nested `{error:{code:"not_found",retryable:false,action:"none"}}` without consulting grants or inventing admission. The production memory shell mounts `GET /v1/device-sessions/ownership` as its own door so native capture preflight is not a UUID session 404. Chat writes, generation SSE, attachments, ownership, and transcription POST stay blocked. Chat copy honors nested `retryable:false` on 503, so a blocked write is unusable-backend copy rather than "Try again." Browser generation SSE returns that same non-retryable 503 immediately instead of reconnecting. Worker `GET /v1/settings` no longer projects `STAGING_*` labels as a profile; D1 admission usage remains. Capture upload retries honor nested `retryable:false` on 503, and `capture_ownership_unavailable` is not a transient upload retry. Library Home Retry / Try again is omitted when every failed saved-data door is nested `retryable:false`. Browser Settings Retry is omitted for nested non-retryable 503s; omitted 503 still retries. ## Verified and pushed

Backend slices include authorized PostgreSQL tasks/audio (a64e477a56), transcription with durable paid-response recovery (f22f1d8572), conversation reads with revision-fenced cursors (8546a0e574), trusted chat context packets (01bdb648bb), GET-only PostgreSQL chat history under an explicit `chat.read` grant, and GET-only Settings that stay unavailable until an owner-backed identity/entitlement producer exists. Chat writes, generation SSE, cancellation and attachments stay explicit nested 404s without admission. Settings identity/entitlement producers and deployed gateway identity composition remain missing.

Native slices include capability-gated LED/microphone controls (9ea1065492), account-session device retirement (9af6d6c248), bounded reconnect plus truthful connecting UI (d2bd77e8b3), and acknowledged Find Device commands (5dd93b4ecd). Native retries use 1/2/4-second delays only after a previously ready connection; manual disconnect, account retirement and radio-off cancel them. Mind Map remains collapsed on Home under the existing design decision. Canonical capture ownership receipts are pushed in b4b989025e; the deletion inventory and real cleanup gate are repaired in 0748acd29b.

Storage status is pushed in f316e7ca5f, nullable unit-aware local Settings in 249feac743, and encrypted recording recovery with the coordinated batch wire in 1563013d22. The root check passed in /tmp/v5-final-journal-settings-check.log: 364 React Native tests, 247 Worker tests, 33 ratified contract tests, 46 PWA tests, native checks and 216 deployed-backend tests. Android, iOS and macOS builds passed in /tmp/v5-storage-*-build.log. PostgreSQL passed 28 tests and 1,029 assertions, restored 52 migrations across 119 tables, and passed pinned Linux Bun/Node parity (/tmp/v5-batch-wire-postgres.log). Independent review caught and verified fixes for packet fsync/acknowledgement ordering, retained-memory accounting and fractional transcription usage.

Conversation pagination is pushed in 9050eddcb5. The real PostgreSQL 18.4 gate passed 28 tests and 11,169 assertions, traversed 10,026 conversations exactly once, restored 53 migrations across 120 tables, and passed Bun/Node runtime parity (/tmp/v5-pagination-postgres-final.log). The root gate passed in /tmp/v5-pagination53-commit-check.log. Migration 53 was applied to DEV after that commit: 52 existing receipts verified, only 53 applied, exactly 53 read back. Double-press saving, automatic rollover, browser capability copy and historical transcript retry are pushed in fa7f0703cd, b3270c6cee, a129a39488 and e62df73dca.

Remembered-device and qualification-runner checkpoints passed the complete root gate (/tmp/v5-remembered-final-root-check.log): 386 React Native tests, 251 Worker tests, 33 ratified contracts, 46 PWA tests, native checks and 224 deployed-backend tests. Final Android/iOS/macOS builds passed in /tmp/v5-remembered-*-build.log. The interrupted-child false-success repair is pushed in 476f330443 and was reproduced and verified with actual SIGINT/SIGTERM children on macOS and pinned Linux Bun 1.3.14.

Tasks pagination across mobile, generic and macOS pages is committed in 38b81af7a1; stale Memories page retirement is committed in e152ef2d1b. The final root gate passed 398 React Native tests with the existing Worker/PWA/native/contract gates and 224 deployed-backend tests (/tmp/v5-task-memory-final-root-check.log). Independent task review passed 70 focused tests. An isolated canonical 26-task service and actual Vite app proxy verified desktop and 390x844 mobile first-page 25 → all 26, then second-page completion → refreshed first page → persisted completion after loading page two. The temporary browser/service were cleaned up. This is local canonical integration proof, not Cloud Run or physical-device proof.

First-audio status is pushed in 4e7c060482: native code retries notifications once after four seconds, preserves quiet links, and reports waiting until a nonempty packet arrives. Firmware connected AAD silence prevents treating quiet as failure. All three native builds and Java/Apple state tests passed (/tmp/v5-first-audio-*-build.log, /tmp/v5-first-audio-native.log); shared UI tests passed. Root checks passed 403 RN tests plus all existing Worker/PWA/native/contract/deployed gates (/tmp/v5-client-qualification-final-check.log).

Actual app recording-client qualification is committed in 70308227dd. The real PostgreSQL suite now invokes the production encoder/parser against the authenticated runtime: lost committed open/audio acknowledgements, exact persisted bytes, replay conflict, foreign identity rejection and once-only synthetic transcription all pass. Final qualification passed 28 tests and 11,182 assertions, restore of 53 migrations across 120 tables and pinned Bun/Node parity (/tmp/v5-client-real-pg-final.log). The pure parser was extracted unchanged; its test dependency loads only inside the real database callback, preserving backend-only Linux skipped-test loading. This does not establish live provider or physical audio proof.

Local identity acceptance now requires exact health/readiness/authorized/unseeded statuses of 200/200/200/403. The official Firebase Auth emulator, Firebase Admin verifier and mounted PostgreSQL memory process passed this check over an owned loopback HTTP socket, including emulator cleanup. Its exclusive lifecycle lease prevents one run from stopping another; shutdown checks the recorded process and process group, and failure cleanup is awaited. Firebase CLI 15.25.1 runs with a child-only Bun PATH so its optional macOS lsof enumeration cannot hang; its real socket checks remain active. Bun 1.4 had intermittent emulator startup failures; the repository-pinned Bun 1.3.14 passed four consecutive start/stop cycles (/tmp/v5-identity-pinned-cycles.log). Empty-account reads use the actual structural renderer with no model calls, and nonempty accounts are rejected by this acceptance harness. This proves local identity/admission only, not production Firebase, rendered memories or deployed app parity. The preceding PostgreSQL qualification again passed 28 tests and 11,182 assertions, 53 migrations/120 restored tables and Bun/Node parity (/tmp/v5-identity-postgres-preserve.log).

The repair is committed in 55a9ac27. Final exact-runtime acceptance passed in /tmp/v5-identity-pinned-live-proof.log. Its preceding pinned PostgreSQL qualification passed in /tmp/v5-identity-pinned-pg.log, and /tmp/v5-identity-pinned-cleanup.log confirms the owned PostgreSQL container and volume were removed. The emulator start command enforces the existing packageManager Bun version; it does not silently attempt the observed unstable runtime. The pinned root gate passed 403 React Native tests, 242 deployed-backend tests and all existing Worker/PWA/native/contract gates (/tmp/v5-identity-pinned-commit-check.log).

Capture-time qualification passed all three native builds and encrypted-journal regressions (/tmp/v5-capture-time-native.log, /tmp/v5-capture-time-{ios,macos,android}-build.log), 145 focused React Native tests and independent backend review. Real PostgreSQL qualification passed 28 tests and 11,199 assertions, restored 54 migrations across 120 tables and passed Linux Bun/Node parity (/tmp/v5-capture-time-postgres.log). Worker qualification passed 253 contract tests and 55 actual D1/R2 tests (/tmp/v5-capture-time-worker.log). The full root gate passed 416 React Native tests, 253 Worker contracts, 55 D1/R2 tests, 33 ratified contracts, 46 PWA tests, native checks and 255 deployed-backend tests (/tmp/v5-capture-time-root-check.log). An isolated canonical local service and actual Vite app rendered different capture/server dates correctly on desktop and 390x844 mobile; mobile document width and scroll width both measured 390. Temporary service/tab cleanup followed. This is browser integration proof; physical BLE capture-time verification remains open.

Durable recording recovery now resumes transient upload/completion failures automatically through one bounded scheduler, preserving fsync ordering, indexed acknowledgements and the shared memory budget. Native backend disposal releases journal handles without deleting saved files and fences late credential/owner publication. Ownership-service HTTP availability errors are retryable without selecting cached authority. Independent review passed 104 recording tests (/tmp/v5-auto-resume-peer.log); all three native builds passed (/tmp/v5-backend-disposal-{android-build,ios-build,macos-build}.log). The frozen-source root gate passed 433 React Native tests, 253 Worker tests, 55 D1/R2 tests, 33 ratified tests, 46 PWA tests, native checks and 274 deployed-backend tests (/tmp/v5-recovery-disposal-root-check.log). Physical offline/background recovery remains unverified.

## Development infrastructure

Only based-hardware-dev was modified. Cloud SQL omi-platform-dev-pg18 runs PostgreSQL 18.4; database omi_platform has migrations 1–54 applied. Dedicated migrator and application-only runtime logins exist. Database URL, codec and cursor secrets have version 1. Secret payloads remain outside the repository and OpenTofu state. The Linux image omi-v5-portable:244d4a4e builds, imports its production module and rejects missing configuration with exit 1 (/tmp/v5-startup-cancel-image-{build,import,unconfigured}.log); initialized service health remains unverified without runtime configuration.

The operator cannot create custom roles or change project/secret IAM. Eight IAM creates remain in the reviewed private plan at ~/.local/share/omi-v5-dev/operator/remaining-iam.tfplan, including access to the existing DEV transcription secret. The operator cannot read that secret, so live transcription-provider verification is blocked before the provider request. The omi-platform-dev Cloud Run service is not deployed. A fresh read-only check confirmed all eight planned IAM resources remain missing: the custom verifier role, two project bindings and five secret-access bindings. The active operator can create/update Cloud Run and act as the runtime service account, but cannot create the role or change the required IAM policies (/tmp/v5-dev-iam-readonly.log). The default gcloud project is production based-hardware; always specify based-hardware-dev.

Migrations 49 through 54 were applied after their verified commits. Migration 54 followed remote commit 3be68aa300: the proxy target and database name were verified, 53 existing receipts matched, only 54 applied, and exactly 54 receipts were read back. The dedicated migration credential and execution logs are private under ~/.local/share/omi-v5-dev/operator. The runtime login was not rotated. Activation still needs legitimate canonical release/account/grant authority; schema installation is not activation.

## Active work and verification limits

Capture-time provenance is implemented across native BLE events, encrypted journals, the recording client, both backend uploads and conversation details. Optional capturedAtMs records the app wall clock at the first nonempty native packet; rollover uses its boundary packet. It survives replay, rejects changed or malformed acknowledgements, and remains absent for legacy journals. Canonical conversation reads expose capturedAtMs; legacy arrays expose captured_at_ms. PostgreSQL migration 54 and Worker migration 9 store it. PostgreSQL session startedAt/endedAt JSON now uses milliseconds, matching Worker responses; server authority, duration, billing and ordering remain unchanged. The detail label is Captured (device time). This is receipt-time provenance, not firmware RTC, original stop time or physical clock accuracy. Migration 54 is applied to DEV after verified commit 3be68aa300. See hardware-device-parity.md. The repaired local Auth-emulator workflow in backends/example-platform/docs/memory-productionization/prod-local-identity.md is explicitly local_test and must never substitute for DEV grants or IAM. It uses its own dynamic HTTP port and preserves the existing port 4851 QA service.

The normal prod-local launcher now uses the shared socket probe and cleanup/render helpers. Construction, startup, bind errors and shutdown signals unwind the same acquired resources; cleanup errors cannot pass as success. Nonempty memory sources fail visibly instead of returning an empty list. Independent review passed 28 focused tests/135 assertions (/tmp/v5-normal-local-peer-review.log), including occupied ephemeral socket ownership, cancellation and error precedence. The existing port 4851 QA service was not replaced; this slice exercises production helpers without claiming a live normal-launcher startup there. Full model rendering remains unsupported in this local launcher.


At 3be68aa300, run 34077565441 passed both backend validation and Apple OAuth callback validation; staging deployment was skipped. Backend validation includes actual recording-client regressions and PostgreSQL integration/runtime parity. Earlier runs exposed two classic Docker image-store incompatibilities and Linux ICU's GMT representation for UTC. The fixes are pushed in ade381dab3, 34f7c2fd1d and 4ebe34e863. Exact pinned Linux formation tests reproduced the UTC failure before repair and passed afterward.

Physical phones were offline. Compilation and synthetic tests do not prove physical BLE writes, link-loss recovery, background capture or firmware operations. Full hardware scope is in hardware-device-parity.md. Background connection configuration is pushed in 62df56426e, but physical delivery and suspension behavior remain unverified. Encrypted recording recovery is implemented and tested, with physical recovery and Android Keystore instrumentation still open. Explicit remembered-device reconnection and Forget are pushed in 1b97a36a4c; physical relaunch/reconnect remains unverified. Storage transfer, firmware and supported camera/motion paths remain incomplete. Double-press conversation saving and automatic frame-boundary rollover are implemented; physical button and long-running capture verification remain open. Storage reads do not advance or erase audio; current firmware notification completion is not a durable app acknowledgement.

The physical iPad named brick became available with Developer Mode enabled. A signed Release iphoneos build with bundled JavaScript installed as the distinct hk.tsc.example app; Omi R4 remains untouched. The device then denied launch because it was locked. The user has been asked to unlock it; do not retry dependent UI actions until it is unlocked. The build artifact is /tmp/v5-ipad-derived/Build/Products/Release-iphoneos/RnRuntime.app, with build/install/launch logs under /tmp/v5-ipad-*.log. The ignored local Xcode environment was restored byte-for-byte. No authentication, consent, recording, or firmware actions occurred, and no portable DEV endpoint or account grant was invented.

Provider-independent chat stores/gateway identity, Settings profile/entitlement producers, attachments, downstream formation workers and other gaps are in provider-agnostic-parity.md. Do not use QA seed identities, fabricated plans, UID-derived activation or empty successful projections to close them. Existing native cloud profile/subscription routes remain available; they do not prove a portable Settings implementation.

An isolated Linux Bun 1.3.14 run passed 2,387 backend tests with 39 explicit skips, zero failures and 16,541 assertions (/tmp/v5-backend-batch52-full.log). It includes the batch-52 cutover and runs with lsof, procps, Python and an init process. Real PostgreSQL qualification runs separately; live model-provider, deployed app and physical-device acceptance remain open.

Apple device replacement now settles a pending storage request before changing peripherals (84862dde51). The regression compiles both actual modules, failed before the fix, and passes stale-response/timeout isolation and new-device storage reads. Both Apple builds and independent review passed. Production startup now unwinds partially acquired resources on construction, bind and readiness errors; explicit shutdown attempts all cleanup and refuses failed or undrained success. Independent review and 14 focused lifecycle tests passed, including a real occupied socket. The full root gate passed with 416 RN and 268 deployed-backend tests plus Worker/D1/R2/PWA/ratified/native checks (/tmp/v5-storage-production-lifecycle-root-check.log). Early startup cancellation is now implemented: signals register before acquisition, PostgreSQL readiness receives cancellation, late acquired handles remain owned until cleanup, and failed cleanup cannot report success. The existing 45-second hard startup watchdog and eight-second signal deadline remain final process bounds; a never-settling dependency still requires forced exit.

Further recovery fixes are pushed in 38b15f78e2 (journal device name remains immutable when discovery learns a name later) and 9fec207040 (Android reconnect settles an interrupted scan). Focused regressions, independent review and Android build passed. Startup cancellation passed 20 tests/56 assertions on pinned macOS and Linux Bun, including real SIGTERM/SIGINT and failed-cleanup child processes (/tmp/v5-production-startup-signal.log, /tmp/v5-startup-cancel-linux.log). The full root gate passed 418 RN tests and 274 deployed-backend tests with existing Worker/D1/R2/PWA/native/ratified gates (/tmp/v5-scan-settlement-commit-check.log). A broader Linux run of committed 7508cad8 stalled inside Bun and was terminated with exit137; its simulator-lease file independently passed 11 tests. The same immutable source passed with built-in file isolation (`bun test --isolate`): 2,425 passed, 39 explicit skips, zero failures and 16,726 assertions across 370 files (/tmp/v5-backend-full-7508-isolated.log). Both owned containers were removed. This qualifies committed 7508cad8; later startup cancellation has separate pinned Linux and root-gate evidence. The runtime stall cause is not established; do not count the original run as a pass.
